Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nAchieves speedup over sequential implementation on multi-core systems.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(doc_chunk: list[Document]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents - compute TF and collect terms.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Must be defined at module level for pickling.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term_freqs_dict, doc_terms_dict, vocabulary_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_tf = {}  # doc_id -> {term: tf}\n    chunk_terms = {}  # doc_id -> set of terms\n    chunk_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc in doc_chunk:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        chunk_tf[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        chunk_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        chunk_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(\n    args: tuple[list[str], dict[int, set[str]]]\n) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_batch, doc_terms_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_batch, doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms_batch:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_posting_lists(\n    args: tuple[list[str], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]\n) -> dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index posting lists for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_batch, doc_term_freqs, idf_scores)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their posting lists\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_batch, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    result = {}\n\n    for term in terms_batch:\n        posting_list = []\n        idf_score = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf_dict[term] * idf_score\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _compute_doc_vectors(\n    args: tuple[list[int], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]\n) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document vectors and norms for a batch of document IDs.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_scores)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Merge partial results\n    3. Compute document frequencies (can be parallelized for large vocabularies)\n    4. Compute IDF scores\n    5. Parallel: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    6. Parallel: Compute document vector norms\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(_process_document_chunk, chunk) for chunk in chunks\n        ]\n\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab = future.result()\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    vocabulary_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n\n    # For smaller vocabularies, compute DF sequentially (less overhead)\n    # For larger vocabularies, parallelize\n    if len(vocabulary_list) < 5000:\n        # Sequential DF computation\n        for term in vocabulary_list:\n            df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n    else:\n        # Parallel DF computation - split vocabulary into chunks\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(500, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = []\n        for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size):\n            vocab_chunks.append(vocabulary_list[i : i + vocab_chunk_size])\n\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n            futures = [\n                executor.submit(_compute_df_for_terms, args) for args in args_list\n            ]\n\n            for future in as_completed(futures):\n                partial_df = future.result()\n                index.document_frequencies.update(partial_df)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Split vocabulary for parallel posting list construction\n    vocab_chunk_size = max(500, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n    vocab_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size):\n        vocab_chunks.append(vocabulary_list[i : i + vocab_chunk_size])\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        args_list = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(_build_posting_lists, args) for args in args_list\n        ]\n\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            partial_index = future.result()\n            index.inverted_index.update(partial_index)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 5: Parallel document vector and norm computation\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    doc_chunk_size = max(500, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        args_list = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(_compute_doc_vectors, args) for args in args_list\n        ]\n\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            partial_vectors, partial_norms = future.result()\n            index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(\n    args: tuple[str, dict, dict, dict, dict, int, dict]\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using the index data.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # Prepare index data for workers (avoiding repeated serialization of full index)\n    idf = index.idf\n    inverted_index = index.inverted_index\n    doc_vectors = index.doc_vectors\n    doc_norms = index.doc_norms\n\n    # Create argument tuples for each query\n    args_list = [\n        (query, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles)\n        for query in queries\n    ]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    results = []\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        # Use map to maintain order\n        results = list(executor.map(_search_single_query, args_list))\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential indexing\n    print(\"\\nSequential indexing...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {seq_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    # Parallel indexing\n    print(f\"\\nParallel indexing ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=args.num_workers\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {par_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    indexing_speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"\\nIndexing speedup: {indexing_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SEARCH COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(f\"\\nSequential batch search ({args.num_queries} queries)...\")\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(\n        queries, seq_result.index, args.top_k, documents\n    )\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential search time: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n\n_worker_idf = None\n_worker_inverted_index = None\n_worker_doc_vectors = None\n_worker_doc_norms = index data.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n    _worker_idf = idf\n    _worker_inverted_index = inverted_index\n    _worker_doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    _worker_doc_norms = doc_norms\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n    _worker_top_k = top_k\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(doc_chunk: list[Document]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents - compute TF and collect terms.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Must be defined at module level for pickling.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term_freqs_dict, doc_terms_dict, vocabulary_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_tf = {}  # doc_id -> {term: tf}\n    chunk_terms = {}  # doc_id -> set of terms\n    chunk_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc in doc_chunk:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        chunk_tf[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        chunk_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        chunk_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab\n\n\ndef _build_posting_lists_and_vectors(\n    args: tuple[list[int], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float], set[str]]\n) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index posting lists AND document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Combines posting list and vector computation to reduce passes over data.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_scores, vocabulary)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (partial_inverted_index, partial_doc_vectors, partial_doc_norms)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf, vocabulary = args\n\n    # Build partial inverted index (only terms that appear in these docs)\n    partial_index = defaultdict(list)\n    partial_vectors = {}\n    partial_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            idf_score = idf.get(term, 0)\n            tfidf = tf * idf_score\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Merge partial results\n    3. Compute document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores\n    5. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n    6. Merge and sort posting lists\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n        for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies (sequential - usually fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Prepare args for each chunk\n    args_list = [\n        (chunk, doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf), index.vocabulary)\n        for chunk in doc_id_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_build_posting_lists_and_vectors, args_list)\n\n        for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n            # Merge inverted index\n            for term, postings in partial_index.items():\n                if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                    index.inverted_index[term] = []\n                index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n            # Merge document vectors and norms\n            index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(_worker_top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing.Pool with initializer to share index data\n    across workers without serializing it for each query.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share index data across workers\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            doc_titles,\n            top_k,\n        ),\n    ) as pool:\n        # Use map to maintain order of results\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query_with_globals, queries)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # 112→        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_scores, vocabulary)\n   113→\n   114→    Returns:\n   115→        Tuple of (partial_inverted_index, partial_doc_vectors, partial_doc_norms)\n   142→# ============================================================================\n   143→# Parallel Index Building\n   144→# ============================================================================\n   145→\n   146→\n   147→def build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n   148→    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n   149→) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n   150→    \"\"\"\n   151→    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n   152→\n   153→    Steps:\n   154→    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n   155→    2. Merge partial results\n   156→    3. Compute document frequencies\n   157→    4. Compute IDF scores\n   158→    5. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n   159→    6. Merge and sort posting lists\n   160→\n   161→    Args:\n   162→        documents: List of Document objects to index\n   163→        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n   164→        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n   165→\n   166→    Returns:\n   167→        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n   168→    \"\"\"\n   169→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   170→\n   171→    if num_workers is None:\n   172→        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n   173→\n   174→    index = TFIDFIndex()\n   175→    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n   176→\n   177→    # ========================================================================\n   199→    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies (sequential - usually fast)\n   200→    # ========================================================================\n   201→\n   202→    for term in index.vocabulary:\n   203→        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n   204→        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n   205→\n   206→    # ========================================================================\n   215→    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n   216→    # ========================================================================\n   217→\n   218→    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n   219→    doc_id_chunks = []\n   220→    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n   221→    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n   222→        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n   223→\n   224→    # Tokenize query\n   279→    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n   280→    if not query_tokens:\n   281→        return []\n   282→\n   283→    # Compute query TF\n   284→    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n   285→\n   286→    # dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index posting lists AND document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Combines posting list and vector computation to reduce passes over data.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_scores, vocabulary)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (partial_inverted_index, partial_doc_vectors, ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Merge partial results\n    3. Compute document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores\n    5. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n    6. Merge and sort posting lists\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n        for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies (sequential - usually fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Prepare args for each chunk\n    args_list = [\n        (chunk, doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf), index.vocabulary)\n        for chunk in doc_id_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_build_posting_lists_and_vectors, args_list)\n\n        for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n            # Merge inverted index\n            for term, postings in partial_index.items():\n                if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                    index.inverted_index[term] = []\n                index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n            # Merge document vectors and norms\n            index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(_worker_top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing.Pool with initializer to share index data\n    across workers without serializing it for each query.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share index data across workers\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            doc_titles,\n            top_k,\n        ),\n    ) as pool:\n        # Use map to maintain order of results\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query_with_globals, queries)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Generate queries\n    queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm optimization\",\n        \"database distributed systems\",\n        \"neural network training\",\n        \"cloud infrastructure security\",\n        \"data analytics pipeline\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n    queries = queries[: args.num_queries]\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"INDEXING COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential indexing\n    print(\"\\nSequential indexing...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {seq_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    # Parallel indexing\n    print(f\"\\nParallel indexing ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=args.num_workers\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {par_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    indexing_speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"\\nIndexing speedup: {indexing_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SEARCH COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(f\"\\nSequential batch search ({args.num_queries} queries)...\")\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(\n        queries, seq_result.index, args.top_k, documents\n    )\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential search time: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    print(f\"\\nParallel batch search ({args.num_queries} queries, {args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_search_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        queries, par_result.index, args.top_k, args.num_workers, documents\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel search time: {par_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    search_speedup = seq_search_time / par_search_time\n    print(f\"\\nSearch speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"CORRECTNESS VERIFICATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    correct = True\n    for i, (seq_res, par_res) in enumerate(\n        zip(seq_search_results, par_search_results)\n    ):\n        if len(seq_res) != len(par_res):\n            print(f\"Query {i}: Result count mismatch!\")\n            correct = False\n            continue\n\n        for j, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(seq_res, par_res)):\n            if s.doc_id != p.doc_id or abs(s.score - p.score) > 1e-9:\n                print(f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Mismatch!\")\n                print(f\"  Sequential: doc_id={s.doc_id}, score={s.score:.6f}\")\n                print(f\"  Parallel:   doc_id={p.doc_id}, score={p.score:.6f}\")\n                correct = False\n\n    if correct:\n        print(\"All results match! Parallel implementation is correct.\")\n    else:\n        print(\"Some results differ. Please investigate.\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Indexing speedup: {indexing_speedup:.2f}x (target: 1.5x)\")\n    print(f\"Search speedup:   {search_speedup:.2f}x (target: 2x)\")\n    print(f\"Targets met: Indexing={'YES' if indexing_speedup >= 1.5 else 'NO'}, \"\n          f\"Search={'YES' if search_speedup >= 2.0 else 'NO'}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":13,"oldLines":7,"newStart":13,"newLines":6,"lines":[" import os"," import time"," from collections import TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nAchieves speedup over sequential implementation on multi-core systems.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(doc_chunk: list[Document]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents - compute TF and collect terms.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Must be defined at module level for pickling.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term_freqs_dict, doc_terms_dict, vocabulary_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_tf = {}  # doc_id -> {term: tf}\n    chunk_terms = {}  # doc_id -> set of terms\n    chunk_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc in doc_chunk:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        chunk_tf[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        chunk_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        chunk_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(\n    args: tuple[list[str], dict[int, set[str]]]\n) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_batch, doc_terms_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_batch, doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms_batch:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_posting_lists(\n    args: tuple[list[str], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]\n) -> dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index posting lists for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_batch, doc_term_freqs, idf_scores)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their posting lists\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_batch, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    result = {}\n\n    for term in terms_batch:\n        posting_list = []\n        idf_score = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf_dict[term] * idf_score\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _compute_doc_vectors(\n    args: tuple[list[int], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]\n) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document vectors and norms for a batch of document IDs.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_scores)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Merge partial results\n    3. Compute document frequencies (can be parallelized for large vocabularies)\n    4. Compute IDF scores\n    5. Parallel: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    6. Parallel: Compute document vector norms\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(_process_document_chunk, chunk) for chunk in chunks\n        ]\n\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab = future.result()\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    vocabulary_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n\n    # For smaller vocabularies, compute DF sequentially (less overhead)\n    # For larger vocabularies, parallelize\n    if len(vocabulary_list) < 5000:\n        # Sequential DF computation\n        for term in vocabulary_list:\n            df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n    else:\n        # Parallel DF computation - split vocabulary into chunks\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(500, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = []\n        for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size):\n            vocab_chunks.append(vocabulary_list[i : i + vocab_chunk_size])\n\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n            futures = [\n                executor.submit(_compute_df_for_terms, args) for args in args_list\n            ]\n\n            for future in as_completed(futures):\n                partial_df = future.result()\n                index.document_frequencies.update(partial_df)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Split vocabulary for parallel posting list construction\n    vocab_chunk_size = max(500, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n    vocab_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size):\n        vocab_chunks.append(vocabulary_list[i : i + vocab_chunk_size])\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        args_list = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(_build_posting_lists, args) for args in args_list\n        ]\n\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            partial_index = future.result()\n            index.inverted_index.update(partial_index)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 5: Parallel document vector and norm computation\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    doc_chunk_size = max(500, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        args_list = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(_compute_doc_vectors, args) for args in args_list\n        ]\n\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            partial_vectors, partial_norms = future.result()\n            index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(\n    args: tuple[str, dict, dict, dict, dict, int, dict]\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using the index data.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # Prepare index data for workers (avoiding repeated serialization of full index)\n    idf = index.idf\n    inverted_index = index.inverted_index\n    doc_vectors = index.doc_vectors\n    doc_norms = index.doc_norms\n\n    # Create argument tuples for each query\n    args_list = [\n        (query, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles)\n        for query in queries\n    ]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    results = []\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        # Use map to maintain order\n        results = list(executor.map(_search_single_query, args_list))\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate queries\n    queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm optimization\",\n        \"database distributed systems\",\n        \"neural network training\",\n        \"cloud infrastructure security\",\n        \"data analytics pipeline\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n    queries = queries[: args.num_queries]\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"INDEXING COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential indexing\n    print(\"\\nSequential indexing...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {seq_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    # Parallel indexing\n    print(f\"\\nParallel indexing ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=args.num_workers\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {par_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    indexing_speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"\\nIndexing speedup: {indexing_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SEARCH COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(f\"\\nSequential batch search ({args.num_queries} queries)...\")\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(\n        queries, seq_result.index, args.top_k, documents\n    )\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential search time: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"CORRECTNESS VERIFICATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    correct = True\n    for i, (seq_res, par_res) in enumerate(\n        zip(seq_search_results, par_search_results)\n    ):\n        if len(seq_res) != len(par_res):\n            print(f\"Query {i}: Result count mismatch!\")\n            correct = False\n            continue\n\n        for j, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(seq_res, par_res)):\n            if s.doc_id != p.doc_id or abs(s.score - p.score) > 1e-9:\n                print(f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Mismatch!\")\n                print(f\"  Sequential: doc_id={s.doc_id}, score={s.score:.6f}\")\n                print(f\"  Parallel:   doc_id={p.doc_id}, score={p.score:.6f}\")\n                correct = False\n\n    if correct:\n        print(\"All results match! ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(doc_chunk: list[Document]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents - compute TF and collect terms.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Must be defined at module level for pickling.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk: List ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies (main cost)\n    2. Sequential: Compute document frequencies (fast for small vocab)\n    3. Sequential: Compute IDF scores\n    4. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n    5. Sequential: Sort posting lists\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF computation)\n    # This is the most expensive phase - text processing is CPU-bound\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel using Pool for better performance\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies (sequential - O(V*D) but fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Prepare data as list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples for workers\n    doc_data = [(doc_id, tf_dict) for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items()]\n\n    # Create chunks of document data\n    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_data_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_data), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_data_chunks.append(doc_data[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share IDF data\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_indexing_worker,\n        initargs=(dict(index.idf),),\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_build_index_for_doc_chunk, doc_data_chunks)\n\n    for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n        # Merge document vectors and norms\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # to amortize pool creation overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers * 5:\n        # Create query batches for each worker\n        batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n        query_batches = []\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size):\n            query_batches.append(queries[i : i + batch_size])\n\n        with Pool(\n            processes=min(num_workers, len(query_batches)),\n            initializer=_init_search_worker,\n            initargs=(\n                index.idf,\n                index.inverted_index,\n                index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms,\n                doc_titles,\n                top_k,\n            ),\n        ) as pool:\n            batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n        # 115→    \"\"\"\n   116→    Build inverted index posting lists AND document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n   117→\n   118→    Uses global _indexing_idf set by initializer.\n   119→\n   120→    Args:\n   121→        doc_chunk_data: List of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n   122→\n   123→    Returns:\n   124→        Tuple of (partial_inverted_index, partial_doc_vectors, partial_doc_norms)\n   125→    \"\"\"\n   126→    global _indexing_idf\n   127→\n   128→    partial_index = defaultdict(list)\n   129→    partial_vectors = {}\n   130→    partial_norms = {}\n   131→\n   132→    for doc_id, partial_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n   142→\n   143→        partial_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n   144→        partial_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n   145→\n   146→    return dict(partial_index), partial_vectors, partial_norms\n   147→\n   148→\n   149→# ============================================================================\n   150→# Parallel Index Building\n   151→# ============================================================================\n   152→\n   153→\n   154→def build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n   155→    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n   156→) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n   157→    \"\"\"\n   158→    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n   159→\n   160→    Strategy:\n   161→    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies (main cost)\n   162→    2. Sequential: Compute document frequencies (fast for small vocab)\n   163→    3. Sequential: Compute IDF scores\n   164→    4. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n   165→    5. Sequential: Sort posting lists\n   166→\n   167→    Args:\n   168→        documents: List of Document objects to index\n   169→        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n   170→        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n   171→\n   172→    Returns:\n   173→        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n   174→    \"\"\"\n   175→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   176→\n   177→    if num_workers is None:\n   178→        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n   179→\n   180→    index = TFIDFIndex()\n   181→    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n   182→\n   183→    # ========================================================================\n   184→    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF computation)\n   185→    # This is the most expensive phase - text processing is CPU-bound\n   186→    # ========================================================================\n   187→\n   188→ Process chunks in parallel using Pool for better performance\n   194→    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n   195→    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n   196→\n   197→    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n   198→        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n   199→\n   200→    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n   201→        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n   202→        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n   203→        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n   204→\n   205→    # ========================================================================\n   206→    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies (sequential - O(V*D) but fast)\n   207→    # ========================================================================\n   208→\n   209→    for term in index.vocabulary:\n   210→        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n   211→        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n   212→\n   213→    # ========================================================================\n   214→    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n   215→    # ========================================================================\n   216→\n   217→    N = index.num_documents\n   218→    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n   219→        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n   220→\n   221→    # ========================================================================\n   222→    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n   223→    # ========================================================================\n   224→\n   225→    # Prepare data as doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n   230→    doc_data_chunks = []\n   231→    for i in range(0, len(doc_data), doc_chunk_size):\n   232→        doc_data_chunks.append(doc_data[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n   233→\n   234→    # Use Pool with initializer to share IDF data\n   235→    with Pool(\n   236→        processes=num_workers,\n   237→        initializer=_init_indexing_worker,\n   238→        initargs=(dict(index.idf),),\n   239→    ) as pool:\n   240→        results = pool.map(_build_index_for_doc_chunk, doc_data_chunks)\n   241→\n   242→    for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n   243→        # Merge inverted index\n   244→        for term, postings in partial_index.items():\n   245→            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n   246→                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n   247→            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n   248→\n   249→        # Merge document vectors and norms\n   250→        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n   251→        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n   252→\n   253→    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n   254→    for term in index.inverted_index:\n   255→        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n   256→\n   257→    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n   258→\n   259→    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n   260→        index=index,\n   261→        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n   262→        num_documents=len(documents),\n   263→        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n   264→    )\n   265→\n   266→\n   267→# ============================================================================\n   268→# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n   269→# ============================================================================\n   270→\n   271→\n   272→def _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n   273→    \"\"\"\n   274→    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n   275→\n   276→    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n   277→\n   278→    Args:\n   279→        query: Search query string\n   280→\n   281→    Returns:\n   282→        List of SearchResult objects\n   283→    \"\"\"\n   284→    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, 296→    query_vector = {}\n   297→    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n   298→    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n   299→        if term in _worker_idf:\n   300→            tfidf = tf * _worker_inverted_index:\n   313→            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n   314→                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n   315→\n   316→    # Compute cosine similarity for each result objects\n   339→    results = []\n   340→    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n   341→        results.append(\n   342→            SearchResult(\n   343→                doc_id=doc_id,\n   344→
