partial_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies (main cost)\n    2. Sequential: Compute document frequencies (fast for small vocab)\n    3. Sequential: Compute IDF scores\n    4. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n    5. Sequential: Sort posting lists\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF computation)\n    # This is the most expensive phase - text processing is CPU-bound\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel using Pool for better performance\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies (sequential - O(V*D) but fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Prepare data as list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples for workers\n    doc_data = [(doc_id, tf_dict) for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items()]\n\n    # Create chunks of document data\n    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_data_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_data), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_data_chunks.append(doc_data[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share IDF data\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_indexing_worker,\n        initargs=(dict(index.idf),),\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_build_index_for_doc_chunk, doc_data_chunks)\n\n    for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n        # Merge inverted index\n        for term, postings in partial_index.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n        # Merge document vectors and norms\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(_worker_top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(queries: list[str]) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    return [_search_single_query_with_globals(q) for q in queries]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing.Pool with initializer to share index data\n    across workers without serializing it for each query.\n\n    For small numbers of queries, we batch them to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # For very small query batches, use batching approach\n    # to amortize pool creation overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers * 5:\n        # Create query batches for each worker\n        batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n        query_batches = []\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size):\n            query_batches.append(queries[i : i + batch_size])\n\n        with Pool(\n            processes=min(num_workers, len(query_batches)),\n            initializer=_init_search_worker,\n            initargs=(\n                index.idf,\n                index.inverted_index,\n                index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms,\n                doc_titles,\n                top_k,\n            ),\n        ) as pool:\n            batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n        # Flatten results\n        results = []\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results.extend(batch)\n    else:\n        # For larger query sets, process queries individually\n        with Pool(\n            processes=num_workers,\n            initializer=_init_search_worker,\n            initargs=(\n                index.idf,\n                index.inverted_index,\n                index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms,\n                doc_titles,\n                top_k,\n            ),\n        ) as pool:\n            results = pool.map(_search_single_query_with_globals, queries)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nAchieves speedup over sequential implementation on multi-core systems.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n\n_worker_idf = None\n_worker_inverted_index = None\n_worker_doc_vectors = None\n_worker_doc_norms = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n_worker_top_k = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n    _worker_idf = idf\n    _worker_inverted_index = inverted_index\n    _worker_doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    _worker_doc_norms = doc_norms\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n    _worker_top_k = top_k\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(doc_chunk: list[Document]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents - compute TF and collect terms.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Must be defined at module level for pickling.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term_freqs_dict, doc_terms_dict, vocabulary_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_tf = {}  # doc_id -> {term: tf}\n    chunk_terms = {}  # doc_id -> set of terms\n    chunk_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc in doc_chunk:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        chunk_tf[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        chunk_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        chunk_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab\n\n\ndef _build_posting_lists_and_vectors(\n    args: tuple[list[int], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float], set[str]]\n) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index posting lists AND document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Combines posting list and vector computation to reduce passes over data.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_scores, vocabulary)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (partial_inverted_index, partial_doc_vectors, partial_doc_norms)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf, vocabulary = args\n\n    # Build partial inverted index (only terms that appear in these docs)\n    partial_index = defaultdict(list)\n    partial_vectors = {}\n    partial_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            idf_score = idf.get(term, 0)\n            tfidf = tf * idf_score\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        partial_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        partial_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_index), partial_vectors, partial_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Merge partial results\n    3. Compute document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores\n    5. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n    6. Merge and sort posting lists\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n        for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies (sequential - usually fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Prepare args for each chunk\n    args_list = [\n        (chunk, doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf), index.vocabulary)\n        for chunk in doc_id_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_build_posting_lists_and_vectors, args_list)\n\n        for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n            # Merge inverted index\n            for term, postings in partial_index.items():\n                if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                    index.inverted_index[term] = []\n                index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n            # Merge document vectors and norms\n            index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(_worker_top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing.Pool with initializer to share index data\n    across workers without serializing it for each query.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share index data across workers\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            doc_titles,\n            top_k,\n        ),\n    ) as pool:\n        # Use map to maintain order of results\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query_with_globals, queries)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Generate queries\n    queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm optimization\",\n        \"database distributed systems\",\n        \"neural network training\",\n        \"cloud infrastructure security\",\n        \"data analytics pipeline\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n    queries = queries[: args.num_queries]\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"INDEXING COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential indexing\n    print(\"\\nSequential indexing...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {seq_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    # Parallel indexing\n    print(f\"\\nParallel indexing ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=args.num_workers\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {par_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    indexing_speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"\\nIndexing speedup: {indexing_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SEARCH COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(f\"\\nSequential batch search ({args.num_queries} queries)...\")\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(\n        queries, seq_result.index, args.top_k, documents\n    )\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential search time: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    print(f\"\\nParallel batch search ({args.num_queries} queries, {args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_search_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        queries, par_result.index, args.top_k, args.num_workers, documents\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel search time: {par_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    search_speedup = seq_search_time / par_search_time\n    print(f\"\\nSearch speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"CORRECTNESS VERIFICATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    correct = True\n    for i, (seq_res, par_res) in enumerate(\n        zip(seq_search_results, par_search_results)\n    ):\n        if len(seq_res) != len(par_res):\n            print(f\"Query {i}: Result count mismatch!\")\n            correct = False\n            continue\n\n        for j, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(seq_res, par_res)):\n            if s.doc_id != p.doc_id or abs(s.score - p.score) > 1e-9:\n                print(f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Mismatch!\")\n                print(f\"  Sequential: doc_id={s.doc_id}, score={s.score:.6f}\")\n                print(f\"  Parallel:   doc_id={p.doc_id}, score={p.score:.6f}\")\n                correct = False\n\n    if correct:\n        print(\"All results match! Parallel implementation is correct.\")\n    else:\n        print(\"Some results differ. Please investigate.\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Indexing speedup: {indexing_speedup:.2f}x (target: 1.5x)\")\n    print(f\"Search speedup:   {search_speedup:.2f}x (target: 2x)\")\n    print(f\"Targets met: Indexing={'YES' if indexing_speedup >= 1.5 else 'NO'}, \"\n          f\"Search={'YES' if search_speedup >= 2.0 else 'NO'}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}

Tokenize all documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for each term\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n\n    Returns:\n        IndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        index.vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n    _worker_idf = idf\n    _worker_inverted_index = inverted_index\n    _worker_doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    _worker_doc_norms = doc_norms\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n    _worker_top_k = ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk_full(\n    doc_chunk: list[Document],\n) -> tuple[dict, set, dict[str, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents - compute TF, collect terms, AND count document frequencies.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Computes partial document frequencies to avoid the O(V*D) sequential scan.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term_freqs_dict, vocabulary_set, partial_doc_freq_count)\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_tf = {}  # doc_id -> {term: tf}\n    chunk_vocab = set()\n    chunk_df = defaultdict(int)  # term -> count of docs containing term in this chunk\n\n terms_in_doc = set(tf.keys())\n        chunk_vocab.update(terms_in_doc)\n\n        # Count each term once per document for DF\n        for term in terms_in_doc:\n            chunk_df[term] += 1\n\n    return chunk_tf, chunk_vocab, dict(chunk_df)\n\n\ndef _build_index_for_doc_chunk(\n    doc_chunk_data: list[tuple[int, dict[str, float]]],\n) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index posting lists AND document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Uses global _indexing_idf set by initializer.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk_data: List of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (partial_inverted_index, partial_doc_vectors, partial_doc_norms)\n    \"\"\"\n    global ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Optimized Strategy:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents, compute TF, AND collect partial DF counts\n    2. Sequential merge: Combine partial results and DF counts (O(V) not O(V*D))\n    3. Sequential: Compute IDF scores (O(V))\n    4. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n    5. Sequential: Sort posting lists\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF + partial DF)\n    # This is the most expensive phase - text processing is CPU-bound\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel using Pool\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk_full, chunks)\n\n    # Merge results - O(sum of chunk sizes) not O(V*D)\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_vocab, chunk_df in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n        # Merge partial document frequencies\n        for term, count in chunk_df.items():\n            if term not in index.document_frequencies:\n                index.document_frequencies[term] = 0\n            index.document_frequencies[term] += count\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Prepare data as list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples for workers\n    doc_data = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    # Create chunks of document data\n    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_data_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_data), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_data_chunks.append(doc_data[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share IDF data\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_indexing_worker,\n        initargs=(dict(index.idf),),\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_build_index_for_doc_chunk, doc_data_chunks)\n\n    for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n        # Merge inverted index\n        for term, postings in partial_index.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n        # Merge document vectors and norms\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(_worker_top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(queries: list[str]) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    return [_search_single_query_with_globals(q) for q in queries]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing.Pool with initializer to share index data\n    across workers without serializing it for each query.\n\n    For small numbers of queries, we batch them to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # Create query batches for each worker\n    # Batching helps amortize pool creation overhead\n    batch_size = max(1, (len(queries) + num_workers - 1) // num_workers)\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size):\n        query_batches.append(queries[i : i + batch_size])\n\n    with Pool(\n        processes=min(num_workers, len(query_batches)),\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            doc_titles,\n            top_k,\n        ),\n    ) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n    # Flatten results\n    results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n_worker_top_k = None\n\n# For indexing workers\n_indexing_idf = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n    _worker_idf = idf\n    _worker_inverted_index = inverted_index\n    _worker_doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    _worker_doc_norms = doc_norms\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n    _worker_top_k = top_k\n\n\ndef _init_indexing_worker(idf):\n    \"\"\"Initialize indexing worker with IDF data.\"\"\"\n    global _indexing_idf\n    _indexing_idf = idf\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk_full(\n    doc_chunk: list[Document],\n) -> tuple[dict, set, dict[str, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents - compute TF, collect terms, AND count document frequencies.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Computes partial document frequencies to avoid the O(V*D) sequential scan.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term_freqs_dict, vocabulary_set, partial_doc_freq_count)\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_tf = {}  # doc_id -> {term: tf}\n    chunk_vocab = set()\n    chunk_df = defaultdict(int)  # term -> count of docs containing term in this chunk\n\n    for doc in doc_chunk:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        chunk_tf[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        terms_in_doc = set(tf.keys())\n        chunk_vocab.update(terms_in_doc)\n\n        # Count each term once per document for DF\n        for term in terms_in_doc:\n            chunk_df[term] += 1\n\n    return chunk_tf, chunk_vocab, dict(chunk_df)\n\n\ndef _build_index_for_doc_chunk(\n    doc_chunk_data: list[tuple[int, dict[str, float]]],\n) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index posting lists AND document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Uses global _indexing_idf set by initializer.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk_data: List of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (partial_inverted_index, partial_doc_vectors, partial_doc_norms)\n    \"\"\"\n    global _indexing_idf\n\n    partial_index = defaultdict(list)\n    partial_vectors = {}\n    partial_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            idf_score = _indexing_idf.get(term, 0)\n            tfidf = tf * idf_score\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        partial_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        partial_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_index), partial_vectors, partial_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Optimized Strategy:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents, compute TF, AND collect partial DF counts\n    2. Sequential merge: Combine partial results and DF counts (O(V) not O(V*D))\n    3. Sequential: Compute IDF scores (O(V))\n    4. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n    5. Sequential: Sort posting lists\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF + partial DF)\n    # This is the most expensive phase - text processing is CPU-bound\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel using Pool\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk_full, chunks)\n\n    # Merge results - O(sum of chunk sizes) not O(V*D)\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_vocab, chunk_df in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n        # Merge partial document frequencies\n        for term, count in chunk_df.items():\n            if term not in index.document_frequencies:\n                index.document_frequencies[term] = 0\n            index.document_frequencies[term] += count\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Prepare data as list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples for workers\n    doc_data = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    # Create chunks of document data\n    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_data_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_data), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_data_chunks.append(doc_data[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share IDF data\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_indexing_worker,\n        initargs=(dict(index.idf),),\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_build_index_for_doc_chunk, doc_data_chunks)\n\n    for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n        # Merge inverted index\n        for term, postings in partial_index.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n        # Merge document vectors and norms\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(_worker_top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(queries: list[str]) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    return [_search_single_query_with_globals(q) for q in queries]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing.Pool with initializer to share index data\n    across workers without serializing it for each query.\n\n    For small numbers of queries, we batch them to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # Create query batches for each worker\n    # Batching helps amortize pool creation overhead\n    batch_size = max(1, (len(queries) + num_workers - 1) // num_workers)\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size):\n        query_batches.append(queries[i : i + batch_size])\n\n    with Pool(\n        processes=min(num_workers, len(query_batches)),\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            doc_titles,\n            top_k,\n        ),\n    ) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n    # Flatten results\n    results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nAchieves speedup over sequential implementation on multi-core systems.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Global variables for worker processes (to avoid serialization overhead)\n# ============================================================================\n\n_worker_idf = None\n_worker_inverted_index = None\n_worker_doc_vectors = None\n_worker_doc_norms = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n_worker_top_k = None\n\n# For indexing workers\n_indexing_idf = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n    _worker_idf = idf\n    _worker_inverted_index = inverted_index\n    _worker_doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    _worker_doc_norms = doc_norms\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n    _worker_top_k = top_k\n\n\ndef _init_indexing_worker(idf):\n    \"\"\"Initialize indexing worker with IDF data.\"\"\"\n    global _indexing_idf\n    _indexing_idf = idf\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(doc_chunk: list[Document]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents - compute TF and collect terms.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Must be defined at module level for pickling.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term_freqs_dict, doc_terms_dict, vocabulary_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_tf = {}  # doc_id -> {term: tf}\n    chunk_terms = {}  # doc_id -> set of terms\n    chunk_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc in doc_chunk:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        chunk_tf[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        chunk_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        chunk_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab\n\n\ndef _build_index_for_doc_chunk(\n    doc_chunk_data: list[tuple[int, dict[str, float]]]\n) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index posting lists AND document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Uses global _indexing_idf set by initializer.\n\n    Args:\n        doc_chunk_data: List of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (partial_inverted_index, partial_doc_vectors, partial_doc_norms)\n    \"\"\"\n    global _indexing_idf\n\n    partial_index = defaultdict(list)\n    partial_vectors = {}\n    partial_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            idf_score = _indexing_idf.get(term, 0)\n            tfidf = tf * idf_score\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        partial_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        partial_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_index), partial_vectors, partial_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallel: Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies (main cost)\n    2. Sequential: Compute document frequencies (fast for small vocab)\n    3. Sequential: Compute IDF scores\n    4. Parallel: Build inverted index AND document vectors simultaneously\n    5. Sequential: Sort posting lists\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel document processing (tokenization + TF computation)\n    # This is the most expensive phase - text processing is CPU-bound\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Create chunks of documents\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel using Pool for better performance\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute document frequencies (sequential - O(V*D) but fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast O(vocab_size))\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel inverted index AND document vector building\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    # Prepare data as list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples for workers\n    doc_data = [(doc_id, tf_dict) for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items()]\n\n    # Create chunks of document data\n    doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_data_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_data), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_data_chunks.append(doc_data[i : i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share IDF data\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_indexing_worker,\n        initargs=(dict(index.idf),),\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_build_index_for_doc_chunk, doc_data_chunks)\n\n    for partial_index, partial_vectors, partial_norms in results:\n        # Merge inverted index\n        for term, postings in partial_index.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n        # Merge document vectors and norms\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_with_globals(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Uses global variables set by _init_search_worker to avoid serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_idf, _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors\n    global _worker_doc_norms, _worker_doc_titles, _worker_top_k\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(_worker_top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(queries: list[str]) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using pre-initialized global index data.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    return [_search_single_query_with_globals(q) for q in queries]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing.Pool with initializer to share index data\n    across workers without serializing it for each query.\n\n    For small numbers of queries, we batch them to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Pre-compute document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # For very small query batches, use batching approach\n    # to amortize pool creation overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers * 5:\n        # Create query batches for each worker\n        batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n        query_batches = []\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size):\n            query_batches.append(queries[i : i + batch_size])\n\n        with Pool(\n            processes=min(num_workers, len(query_batches)),\n            initializer=_init_search_worker,\n            initargs=(\n                index.idf,\n                index.inverted_index,\n                index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms,\n                doc_titles,\n                top_k,\n            ),\n        ) as pool:\n            batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n        # Flatten results\n        results = []\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results.extend(batch)\n    else:\n        # For larger query sets, process queries individually\n        with Pool(\n            processes=num_workers,\n            initializer=_init_search_worker,\n            initargs=(\n                index.idf,\n                index.inverted_index,\n                index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms,\n                doc_titles,\n                top_k,\n            ),\n        ) as pool:\n            results = pool.map(_search_single_query_with_globals, queries)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import batch_search_sequential, build_tfidf_index_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries to run\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results per query\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Generate queries\n    queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm optimization\",\n        \"database distributed systems\",\n        \"neural network training\",\n        \"cloud infrastructure security\",\n        \"data analytics pipeline\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n    queries = queries[: args.num_queries]\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"INDEXING COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential indexing\n    print(\"\\nSequential indexing...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {seq_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    # Parallel indexing\n    print(f\"\\nParallel indexing ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=args.num_workers\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {par_result.vocabulary_size:,}\")\n\n    indexing_speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"\\nIndexing speedup: {indexing_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SEARCH COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(f\"\\nSequential batch search ({args.num_queries} queries)...\")\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(\n        queries, seq_result.index, args.top_k, documents\n    )\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential search time: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    print(f\"\\nParallel batch search ({args.num_queries} queries, {args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_search_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        queries, par_result.index, args.top_k, args.num_workers, documents\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel search time: {par_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    search_speedup = seq_search_time / par_search_time\n    print(f\"\\nSearch speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"CORRECTNESS VERIFICATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    correct = True\n    for i, (seq_res, par_res) in enumerate(\n        zip(seq_search_results, par_search_results)\n    ):\n        if len(seq_res) != len(par_res):\n            print(f\"Query {i}: Result count mismatch!\")\n            correct = False\n            continue\n\n        for j, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(seq_res, par_res)):\n            if s.doc_id != p.doc_id or abs(s.score - p.score) > 1e-9:\n                print(f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Mismatch!\")\n                print(f\"  Sequential: doc_id={s.doc_id}, score={s.score:.6f}\")\n                print(f\"  Parallel:   doc_id={p.doc_id}, score={p.score:.6f}\")\n                correct = False\n\n    if correct:\n        print(\"All results match! Parallel implementation is correct.\")\n    else:\n        print(\"Some results differ. Please investigate.\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Indexing speedup: {indexing_speedup:.2f}x (target: 1.5x)\")\n    print(f\"Search speedup:   {search_speedup:.2f}x (target: 2x)\")\n    print(f\"Targets met: Indexing={'YES' if indexing_speedup >= 1.5 else 'NO'}, \"\n          f\"Search={'YES' if search_speedup >= 2.0 else 'NO'}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}
